# Plugins · T · part 5

1,360 entries, todo-stack to traffic-light. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **todo-stack**: Adds a todo card to the session pane that keeps every todo the model creates on one stack shared by every session, newest on top, so none is lost to compaction, a reload or a new session; the model reads and edits it through a todos tool.
